In `src/magent/remote_mux.py`, after `provision_node` (Task 12A):

```python
SETUP_TIMEOUT_S = 900.0


def setup_node(
    node: Node, users: Sequence[str], pubkey: str, *, timeout_s: float
) -> ProvisionReport:
    """Run setup.sh as ``root@<host>`` -- the one root hop. ``users`` are the
    Unix users to create (argv; setup.sh validates them before it changes
    anything), ``pubkey`` is this PC's public key (the payload). Each user's
    node GitHub key comes back as a ``key`` row: ``report.keys()``."""
    root = dataclasses.replace(node, user="root")
    result = run_script(
        root,
        "setup",
        list(users),
        timeout_s=timeout_s,
        stdin=(pubkey.strip() + "\n").encode("utf-8"),
        check=False,
    )
    return _report_of(result, "setup", root)
```

---

### Task 14: `doctor.sh` and `remote_mux.doctor`: read-only rows, always exit 0

`doctor.sh` runs as the node user. It prints one row per check and exits 0 whatever it finds, because a finding is a row and not an exit code. The exit-code decision belongs to `magent node doctor` (Task 16).

| item | ok | not ok |
|---|---|---|
| `tmux` | on PATH at the 3.2 floor or newer (DECISION-22; detail: `tmux -V`) | `fail`: missing is "… -- run: magent node setup"; older than 3.2 or unreadable names the version and the floor, because D's `bring_up.sh` refuses it (rc 4). The predicate is Task 13's shared `tmux_floor.sh`. |
| `git`, `claude`, `python3` | on PATH (detail: first line of its version) | `fail`, "… -- run: magent node setup" |
| `gh` | on PATH | `warn` (provision's `gh` row already fails on it) |
| `claude-login` | `claude auth status --json` says `"loggedIn": true` | `fail`, "… -- run once: ssh `<target>` claude"; `skip` when claude is missing |
| `github-key` | `ssh -T git@github.com` answers `Hi <login>!` | `fail`, with ssh's last line |
| `locale` | `locale charmap` is `UTF-8` | `warn` |
| `disk` | ≥ 5 GB free under the node's root (`~` expanded; a root that does not exist yet is measured at its nearest existing parent) | `warn` below 5 GB, `fail` below 1 GB, `warn` when `df` cannot say |
| `sessions` | always `ok`: the count on the tmux socket `run_script` passed (`remote_mux.SOCKET`) | — |

The `github-key` probe is the one check that can write: on first contact ssh records github.com's host key in `~/.ssh/known_hosts` (`StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new`), exactly as the first `git clone` would.

`--target` carries the ssh target the PC uses (`node.target`), so the `claude-login` repair names the exact command to run. The node cannot know which alias the PC uses for it.

**The socket is a REQUIRED argument, never a default (DECISION-26 ii).** `doctor.sh` has no `--socket` option and no `socket=magent`: like every entry script it includes B's `lib.sh`, which reads `$1` (the socket `run_script` always passes from `remote_mux.SOCKET`) into `MAGENT_SOCKET` with no default and shifts it off. `check_sessions` counts `tmux -L "$MAGENT_SOCKET" list-sessions`. A doctor run without a socket fails loudly in lib.sh before any probe. Pinned three ways: the literal (as D pins `bring_up.sh`), a real-bash run with a socket that is NOT `remote_mux.SOCKET`, and a run with none.

**Files:**
- Create: `src/magent/node_scripts/doctor.sh`.
- Modify: `src/magent/remote_mux.py`: `DOCTOR_TIMEOUT_S` and `doctor` after `setup_node`.
- Test: `tests/unit/test_node_provision.py` (append; add `import re` to the imports if absent).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (append)

```python
DOCTOR_TOOLS = ("bash", "awk", "dirname", "head", "wc")
NODE_TOOLS = ("tmux", "git", "claude", "python3", "gh", "ssh", "locale", "df")
GIB_KB = 1024 * 1024
HI = "Hi alice! You've successfully authenticated, but GitHub does not provide shell access."
DOCTOR_ITEMS = (
    "tmux",
    "git",
    "claude",
    "python3",
    "gh",
    "claude-login",
    "github-key",
    "locale",
    "disk",
    "sessions",
)


def _df(avail_kb: int) -> str:
    return (
        "Filesystem 1024-blocks Used Available Capacity Mounted on\n"
        f"/dev/sda1 104857600 1048576 {avail_kb} 2% /\n"
    )


def _doctor_box(
    tmp_path: Path,
    *,
    tools: tuple[str, ...] = NODE_TOOLS,
    logged_in: bool = True,
    github: str = HI,
    charmap: str = "UTF-8",
    avail_kb: int = 50 * GIB_KB,
    tmux_version: str = "tmux 3.4",
) -> tuple[dict[str, FakeSsh], dict[str, str]]:
    """A node user's home and a PATH of fakes answering like a healthy node,
    except where a keyword says otherwise."""
    fakes = {name: make_fake_ssh(tmp_path, name=name) for name in tools}
    replies = {
        "tmux": [
            ("-V", tmux_version + "\n"),
            ("list-sessions", "a: 1 windows\nb: 1 windows\n"),
        ],
        "claude": [("auth status", json.dumps({"loggedIn": logged_in}) + "\n")],
        "locale": [("charmap", charmap + "\n")],
        "df": [("-Pk", _df(avail_kb))],
    }
    for name, fake in fakes.items():
        for match, stdout in replies.get(name, []):
            fake.set_reply(match, stdout=stdout)
    if "ssh" in fakes:
        fakes["ssh"].set_reply("git@github.com", stderr=github + "\n", rc=1)
    (tmp_path / "node" / "magent").mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    sysbin = _sysbin(tmp_path, DOCTOR_TOOLS, python=False, name="doctorbin")
    env = {
        "HOME": str(tmp_path / "node"),
        "PATH": os.pathsep.join([*(str(f.base) for f in fakes.values()), str(sysbin)]),
    }
    return fakes, env


def _run_doctor(
    env: dict[str, str],
    root: str = "~/magent",
    *,
    socket: str | None = remote_mux.SOCKET,
) -> subprocess.CompletedProcess[bytes]:
    args = ["--root", root, "--target", "alice@box-second"]
    return subprocess.run(
        _bash_argv(*args, socket=socket),
        input=remote_mux._frame_script(node_scripts.script("doctor"), None),
        capture_output=True,
        env=env,
        timeout=60,
        check=False,
    )


@POSIX_BASH
class TestDoctorShUnderRealBash:
    def test_a_healthy_node_is_all_ok(self, tmp_path):
        _, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path)
        r = _run_doctor(env)
        assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
        assert _rows(r) == dict.fromkeys(DOCTOR_ITEMS, "ok")
        details = {line.item: line.detail for line in _report(r).lines}
        assert details["github-key"] == "authenticates as alice"
        assert details["sessions"] == f"2 on tmux socket {remote_mux.SOCKET}"
        assert details["tmux"] == "tmux 3.4"

    def test_the_socket_argument_names_the_tmux_server_probed(self, tmp_path):
        # Not remote_mux.SOCKET: proves the socket is read, never baked in.
        fakes, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path)
        r = _run_doctor(env, socket="mgtest")
        assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
        assert ["-L", "mgtest", "list-sessions"] in [
            c.argv for c in fakes["tmux"].calls()
        ]
        details = {line.item: line.detail for line in _report(r).lines}
        assert details["sessions"] == "2 on tmux socket mgtest"

    def test_no_socket_fails_loudly_before_any_probe(self, tmp_path):
        # lib.sh's ${1:?}: no default server is ever guessed.
        fakes, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path)
        r = _run_doctor(env, socket=None)
        assert r.returncode != 0
        assert b"the tmux socket name is a required first argument" in r.stderr
        assert r.stdout == b""
        assert all(f.calls() == [] for f in fakes.values())

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        ("version", "status"),
        [
            ("tmux 3.0a", "fail"),  # Ubuntu 20.04's: bring_up.sh refuses it
            ("tmux 3.2a", "ok"),
            ("tmux next-3.5", "ok"),
            ("tmux master", "fail"),
        ],
    )
    def test_tmux_is_held_to_the_bring_up_floor(self, tmp_path, version, status):
        _, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path, tmux_version=version)
        r = _run_doctor(env)
        assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
        (row,) = [line for line in _report(r).lines if line.item == "tmux"]
        assert row.status == status
        assert version in row.detail
        if status == "fail":
            assert "3.2 or newer" in row.detail

    def test_a_missing_tool_fails_but_a_missing_gh_only_warns(self, tmp_path):
        tools = tuple(t for t in NODE_TOOLS if t not in ("git", "gh"))
        _, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path, tools=tools)
        rows = _rows(_run_doctor(env))
        assert (rows["git"], rows["gh"]) == ("fail", "warn")

    def test_a_node_not_logged_in_names_the_one_command_that_fixes_it(self, tmp_path):
        _, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path, logged_in=False)
        report = _report(_run_doctor(env))
        (row,) = [line for line in report.lines if line.item == "claude-login"]
        assert row.status == "fail"
        assert row.detail.endswith("run once: ssh alice@box-second claude")

    def test_a_refused_github_key_fails(self, tmp_path):
        _, env = _doctor_box(
            tmp_path, github="git@github.com: Permission denied (publickey)."
        )
        report = _report(_run_doctor(env))
        (row,) = [line for line in report.lines if line.item == "github-key"]
        assert row.status == "fail"
        assert "Permission denied (publickey)." in row.detail

    @pytest.mark.parametrize(
        ("avail_kb", "status"),
        [(512 * 1024, "fail"), (2 * GIB_KB, "warn"), (50 * GIB_KB, "ok")],
    )
    def test_free_disk_under_the_root_is_graded(self, tmp_path, avail_kb, status):
        _, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path, avail_kb=avail_kb)
        assert _rows(_run_doctor(env))["disk"] == status

    def test_a_locale_that_is_not_utf8_warns(self, tmp_path):
        _, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path, charmap="ANSI_X3.4-1968")
        assert _rows(_run_doctor(env))["locale"] == "warn"

    def test_the_root_is_measured_under_home(self, tmp_path):
        fakes, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path)
        _run_doctor(env)
        (call,) = fakes["df"].calls()
        assert call.argv == ["-Pk", str(tmp_path / "node" / "magent")]

    def test_a_root_not_created_yet_is_measured_at_its_nearest_parent(self, tmp_path):
        fakes, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path)
        _run_doctor(env, root="~/magent/not/yet")
        (call,) = fakes["df"].calls()
        assert call.argv == ["-Pk", str(tmp_path / "node" / "magent")]

    def test_an_empty_node_still_exits_zero_with_a_row_per_check(self, tmp_path):
        _, env = _doctor_box(tmp_path, tools=())
        r = _run_doctor(env)
        assert r.returncode == 0, r.stderr
        assert _rows(r) == {
            "tmux": "fail",
            "git": "fail",
            "claude": "fail",
            "python3": "fail",
            "gh": "warn",
            "claude-login": "skip",
            "github-key": "fail",
            "locale": "warn",
            "disk": "warn",
            "sessions": "ok",
        }
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** Create `src/magent/node_scripts/doctor.sh` (LF line endings, trailing newline):

```bash
#!/usr/bin/env bash
# magent node doctor: read-only health rows for this node user.
# Fed over stdin by remote_mux.doctor
# (`bash -s -- <socket> --root R --target T`); reads no payload. lib.sh takes
# the socket (required, no default) into MAGENT_SOCKET.
# Always exits 0: a finding is a row, and magent node doctor owns the exit code.
# No -e: every probe's failure is a finding, not a reason to stop.
set -uo pipefail
# @include lib.sh
# @include tmux_floor.sh

GIB_KB=1048576  # df -Pk reports KiB

say() { printf '%s\t%s\t%s\n' "$1" "$2" "${3:-}"; }

# check_tool <name> <status when missing> <version argv...>
check_tool() {
  local name=$1 missing=$2
  shift 2
  if command -v "$name" >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    say ok "$name" "$("$@" 2>&1 | head -n1)"
  else
    say "$missing" "$name" "$name is not on PATH -- run: magent node setup"
  fi
}

# tmux is on PATH AND at bring_up.sh's floor (DECISION-22): an old tmux is a
# fail, because the first bring-up would refuse it with rc 4.
check_tmux() {
  local verdict version floor
  verdict=$(magent_tmux_verdict)
  version=${verdict#*$'\t'}
  floor=$(magent_tmux_floor)
  case ${verdict%%$'\t'*} in
    ok) say ok tmux "$version" ;;
    missing) say fail tmux "tmux is not on PATH -- run: magent node setup" ;;
    unread) say fail tmux "cannot read the tmux version ($version); magent needs tmux $floor or newer" ;;
    *) say fail tmux "$version is too old; magent needs tmux $floor or newer -- upgrade tmux on this node" ;;
  esac
}

check_claude_login() {
  local target=$1 out
  if ! command -v claude >/dev/null 2>&1; then
    say skip claude-login "claude is not installed"
    return
  fi
  out=$(claude auth status --json 2>/dev/null) || true
  if [[ $out =~ \"loggedIn\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*true ]]; then
    say ok claude-login "logged in"
  else
    say fail claude-login "Claude Code is not logged in here -- run once: ssh $target claude"
  fi
}

check_github_key() {
  local out
  out=$(ssh -T -o BatchMode=yes -o ConnectTimeout=10 \
    -o StrictHostKeyChecking=accept-new git@github.com 2>&1) || true
  if [[ $out =~ Hi\ ([^!]+)! ]]; then
    say ok github-key "authenticates as ${BASH_REMATCH[1]}"
  else
    say fail github-key "GitHub refused this node's key (${out##*$'\n'}) -- run: magent node setup"
  fi
}

check_locale() {
  local charmap
  charmap=$(locale charmap 2>/dev/null) || charmap=""
  if [ "$charmap" = UTF-8 ]; then
    say ok locale "UTF-8"
  else
    say warn locale "charmap is ${charmap:-unknown}, not UTF-8 -- set LANG=C.UTF-8 for this user"
  fi
}

check_disk() {
  local root=$1 dir avail
  dir=${root/#\~/$HOME}
  while [ ! -d "$dir" ] && [ "$dir" != / ] && [ "$dir" != . ]; do
    dir=$(dirname "$dir")
  done
  avail=$(df -Pk "$dir" 2>/dev/null | awk 'NR == 2 {print $4}') || avail=""
  if ! [[ $avail =~ ^[0-9]+$ ]]; then
    say warn disk "could not read the free space under $root"
  elif [ "$avail" -lt "$GIB_KB" ]; then
    say fail disk "$((avail / 1024)) MB free under $root"
  elif [ "$avail" -lt $((5 * GIB_KB)) ]; then
    say warn disk "$((avail / GIB_KB)) GB free under $root"
  else
    say ok disk "$((avail / GIB_KB)) GB free under $root"
  fi
}

check_sessions() {
  local n
  n=$(tmux -L "$MAGENT_SOCKET" list-sessions 2>/dev/null | wc -l) || n=0
  say ok sessions "$((n)) on tmux socket $MAGENT_SOCKET"
}

main() {
  local root=$HOME target="this node"
  while [ "$#" -gt 0 ]; do
    case "$1" in
      --root | --target)
        if [ "$#" -lt 2 ]; then
          say fail doctor "$1 needs a value"
          return 0
        fi
        case "$1" in
          --root) root=$2 ;;
          --target) target=$2 ;;
        esac
        shift 2
        ;;
      *)
        say fail doctor "unknown argument: $1"
        return 0
        ;;
    esac
  done
  exec </dev/null
  export PATH="$HOME/.local/bin:$PATH"
  check_tmux
  check_tool git fail git --version
  check_tool claude fail claude --version
  check_tool python3 fail python3 --version
  check_tool gh warn gh --version
  check_claude_login "$target"
  check_github_key
  check_locale
  check_disk "$root"
  check_sessions
  return 0
}

main "$@"; exit $?
```

In `src/magent/remote_mux.py`, after `setup_node`:

```python
DOCTOR_TIMEOUT_S = 30.0


def doctor(node: Node, *, timeout_s: float) -> ProvisionReport:
    """doctor.sh's rows for ``node``. The script is read-only and exits 0
    whatever it finds; ssh's own failure (255) raises, and an unreachable
    node is the caller's row to print. The tmux socket is not an argument
    here: run_script passes ``SOCKET`` first on every call (DECISION-26 ii)."""
    result = run_script(
        node,
        "doctor",
        ["--root", node.root, "--target", node.target],
        timeout_s=timeout_s,
        check=False,
    )
    return _report_of(result, "doctor", node)
```

### Task 15: `register_ssh_key`: a node's key onto this PC's GitHub account, once

`setup.sh` generates each node user's own ed25519 key and reports its public half in a `key` row. `register_ssh_key` adds that public key to the GitHub account of this PC's `gh`, as an authentication key. It returns ONE `github-key` row and never raises.
- No gh login on this PC: `fail`, naming `gh auth login`.
- A login without `admin:public_key` or `write:public_key` (this machine's case, F4): `fail`, naming `gh auth refresh -h github.com -s admin:public_key`.
- The key is already on the account (`gh api --paginate user/keys`): `skip`. Nothing is added twice.
- Otherwise `gh ssh-key add - --title <title> --type authentication`, with the key on stdin: `did`, or `fail` with gh's last stderr line.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/magent/remote_mux.py`: `register_ssh_key` after `doctor`.
- Test: `tests/unit/test_node_provision.py` (append).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests** (append)

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `src/magent/remote_mux.py`, after `doctor`:

```python
# Either scope lets gh add an ssh key; admin: is what `gh auth refresh` grants.
SSH_KEY_SCOPES = frozenset({"admin:public_key", "write:public_key"})


def register_ssh_key(pubkey: str, *, title: str) -> ScriptLine:
    """Add a node's public key to this PC's GitHub account (gh, authentication
    key), once: a key already on the account is a skip. One ``github-key``
    row; never raises. The key is public, but it rides stdin anyway."""
    account = local_gh_account()
    if account is None:
        return ScriptLine(
            "fail", "github-key", "gh is not logged in on this PC: gh auth login"
        )
    if not account.scopes & SSH_KEY_SCOPES:
        return ScriptLine(
            "fail",
            "github-key",
            (
                f"this PC's gh login ({account.login}) cannot add ssh keys: "
                "gh auth refresh -h github.com -s admin:public_key"
            ),
        )
    parts = pubkey.split()
    if len(parts) < 2:
        return ScriptLine("fail", "github-key", "not an ssh public key line")
    listed = _gh(["api", "--paginate", "user/keys", "--jq", ".[].key"])
    if listed is not None and listed.returncode == 0:
        if parts[1] in listed.stdout.decode("utf-8", "replace").split():
            return ScriptLine(
                "skip", "github-key", f"already registered to {account.login}"
            )
    added = _gh(
        ["ssh-key", "add", "-", "--title", title, "--type", "authentication"],
        input_bytes=(" ".join(parts) + "\n").encode("utf-8"),
    )
    if added is None:
        return ScriptLine("fail", "github-key", "gh could not run on this PC")
    if added.returncode != 0:
        err = added.stderr.decode("utf-8", "replace").strip().splitlines()
        detail = err[-1][:200] if err else f"exited {added.returncode}"
        return ScriptLine("fail", "github-key", f"gh ssh-key add failed: {detail}")
    return ScriptLine(
        "did", "github-key", f"registered to {account.login} as {title!r}"
    )
```

### Task 16: `magent node doctor [<nick>]`

`node doctor` prints, for each node (one given nick, or every node in config order):
- the node's own rows: `doctor.sh` over ONE ssh call (Task 14). An unreachable node becomes one `fail reach` row naming its target and ssh's last stderr line.
- this PC's rows:
  - `sync-daemon`: the `node-sync` heartbeat. Fresh is `ok`, stale is `warn`, and stopped is `warn` when a project runs on a node, else `skip`.
  - `snapshot`: E's `nodes.read_sessions(nick)`, judged by E's `nodes.sessions_stale`. Within 2 x `pullIntervalS` is `ok`, older is `warn` (its sessions read `stale`, never dead), and none yet (or not a snapshot) is `skip`. F does not parse `sessions.json` itself.

The nodes are checked concurrently, so N nodes cost one `DOCTOR_TIMEOUT_S`, not N.

Exit codes: 0 when nothing failed (warnings allowed), 1 when any row failed, 2 when the nick is not in `settings.nodes` (the message is `node_for_nick`'s, word for word). A config that cannot load exits 1 through `_load_config_or_exit`. `--json` prints `{"ok": true, "failures": N, "nodes": {nick: [{status, item, detail}, ...]}}` on stdout with the same exit codes; an unknown nick prints `{"ok": false, "error": ...}`.

The functions `node_checks` and `sync_lines` are public because `magent doctor`'s `nodes` row (Task 18) folds the same rows.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/magent/cli/node_cmd.py` (created by PR-E; see F1).
- Modify: `src/magent/cli/docs.py`: the `magent node doctor [<nick>]` row.
- Test: `tests/unit/test_node_cmd.py`. Create it if E and G have not; otherwise append, and merge the imports.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

```python
"""magent node setup / node doctor: exit codes, rows, and what reaches ssh/gh."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import os
import time

import pytest

from magent import cli, log, node_sync, nodes
from magent.cli import node_cmd
from magent.config import SCHEMA_VERSION, load_config
from magent.remote_mux import ScriptLine

HEALTHY = "ok\ttmux\ttmux 3.4\nok\tclaude-login\tlogged in\n"
NODE_PROJECT = {"path": "api", "node": "second"}


def _pool_file(tmp_config, nicks=("second",), projects=()) -> str:
    return tmp_config(
        {
            "version": SCHEMA_VERSION,
            "settings": {
                "nodes": {n: {"host": f"box-{n}", "user": "alice"} for n in nicks}
            },
            "projects": list(projects),
        }
    )


def _snapshot(nick: str, ts: object) -> None:
    # The shape E's nodes.read_sessions accepts: a numeric ts and a list.
    nodes.write_json_atomic(nodes.sessions_path(nick), {"ts": ts, "sessions": []})


def _heartbeat(age_s: float = 0.0) -> None:
    # The name comes from E's node_sync, the one _daemon_state() reads
    # (DECISION-17); a literal here would pass even if the two drifted.
    log.write_heartbeat(node_sync.HEARTBEAT_NAME)
    if age_s:
        old = time.time() - age_s
        path = log.HEARTBEAT_DIR / f"{node_sync.HEARTBEAT_NAME}.heartbeat"
        os.utime(path, (old, old))


def _sync(tmp_config, projects=()) -> list[ScriptLine]:
    cfg = load_config(_pool_file(tmp_config, projects=projects))
    return node_cmd.sync_lines(cfg, "second", now=time.time())


def _doctor(runner, cfg: str, *args: str):
    return runner.invoke(cli.main, ["--config", cfg, "node", "doctor", *args])


class TestThisPcsSyncRows:
    def test_a_running_daemon_is_ok(self, tmp_config):
        _heartbeat()
        assert _sync(tmp_config)[0] == ScriptLine("ok", "sync-daemon", "running")

    def test_a_stale_heartbeat_warns(self, tmp_config):
        _heartbeat(age_s=log.HEARTBEAT_MAX_AGE + 60)
        assert _sync(tmp_config)[0].status == "warn"

    def test_a_stopped_daemon_warns_while_a_project_runs_on_a_node(self, tmp_config):
        assert _sync(tmp_config, projects=[NODE_PROJECT])[0].status == "warn"

    def test_a_stopped_daemon_is_a_skip_when_no_project_uses_a_node(self, tmp_config):
        assert _sync(tmp_config)[0] == ScriptLine(
            "skip", "sync-daemon", "no project runs on a node"
        )

    def test_a_fresh_snapshot_is_ok(self, tmp_config):
        _snapshot("second", time.time() - 5)
        assert _sync(tmp_config)[1] == ScriptLine("ok", "snapshot", "pulled 5s ago")

    def test_a_snapshot_older_than_two_pulls_warns_and_names_the_limit(
        self, tmp_config
    ):
        _snapshot("second", time.time() - 61)
        row = _sync(tmp_config)[1]
        assert row.status == "warn"
        assert "(60s)" in row.detail

    def test_no_snapshot_yet_is_a_skip(self, tmp_config):
        assert _sync(tmp_config)[1].status == "skip"

    @pytest.mark.parametrize("ts", ["yesterday", True, None])
    def test_a_snapshot_without_a_numeric_ts_is_a_skip(self, tmp_config, ts):
        _snapshot("second", ts)
        assert _sync(tmp_config)[1].status == "skip"


class TestNodeDoctor:
    def test_a_healthy_node_exits_zero_and_prints_its_rows(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh
    ):
        fake_ssh.set_reply("bash -s", stdout=HEALTHY)
        _heartbeat()
        _snapshot("second", time.time())
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config))
        assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
        assert "tmux 3.4" in result.stdout
        assert "No failures." in result.stdout

    def test_a_failed_row_exits_one(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        fake_ssh.set_reply(
            "bash -s",
            stdout="fail\tclaude-login\tClaude Code is not logged in here -- run once: ssh alice@box-second claude\n",
        )
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config))
        assert result.exit_code == 1
        assert "run once: ssh alice@box-second claude" in result.stdout

    def test_warnings_alone_exit_zero(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        fake_ssh.set_reply("bash -s", stdout="warn\tlocale\tcharmap is POSIX\n")
        assert _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config)).exit_code == 0

    def test_an_unreachable_node_is_a_failed_row_naming_its_target(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh
    ):
        fake_ssh.set_reply(
            "bash -s",
            stderr="ssh: connect to host box-second port 22: Connection refused\n",
            rc=255,
        )
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config))
        assert result.exit_code == 1
        assert (
            "cannot reach alice@box-second: ssh: connect to host box-second "
            "port 22: Connection refused"
        ) in result.stdout

    def test_a_nick_checks_only_that_node(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config, ("second", "fifth")), "fifth")
        (call,) = fake_ssh.calls()
        assert "alice@box-fifth" in call.argv

    def test_no_nick_checks_every_node_in_config_order(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh
    ):
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config, ("second", "fifth")), "--json")
        assert list(json.loads(result.stdout)["nodes"]) == ["second", "fifth"]
        assert len(fake_ssh.calls()) == 2

    def test_an_unknown_nick_exits_two_and_names_the_pool(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh
    ):
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "nope")
        assert result.exit_code == 2
        assert "node 'nope' is not in settings.nodes (known: second)" in result.output
        assert fake_ssh.calls() == []

    def test_no_nodes_configured_says_how_to_add_one(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh
    ):
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config, ()))
        assert result.exit_code == 0
        assert "no nodes configured" in result.stdout
        assert fake_ssh.calls() == []

    def test_json_carries_every_row_in_order(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        fake_ssh.set_reply("bash -s", stdout=HEALTHY)
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "--json")
        assert result.exit_code == 0
        payload = json.loads(result.stdout)
        assert (payload["ok"], payload["failures"]) == (True, 0)
        rows = payload["nodes"]["second"]
        assert rows[0] == {"status": "ok", "item": "tmux", "detail": "tmux 3.4"}
        assert [r["item"] for r in rows] == [
            "tmux",
            "claude-login",
            "sync-daemon",
            "snapshot",
        ]

    def test_json_counts_failures_and_exits_one(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        fake_ssh.set_reply("bash -s", stdout="fail\tgit\tgit is not on PATH\n")
        result = _doctor(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "--json")
        assert result.exit_code == 1
        assert json.loads(result.stdout)["failures"] == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `src/magent/cli/node_cmd.py`, merge these into the top-level imports (keep what E has):

```python
import dataclasses
import json
import sys
import time
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, NoReturn

import click

from magent import env, nodes
from magent.cli.config_io import _load_config_or_exit
from magent.config import runs_on_node
from magent.paths import find_config
from magent.style import style

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from collections.abc import Sequence

    from magent.config import MagentConfig
    from magent.remote_mux import RemoteError, ScriptLine
```

`remote_mux` (ssh, tar, the payload builder) is a heavy subsystem, so `node_cmd` imports it IN-BODY only (DECISION-26 v): every function below that constructs a `ScriptLine` or dials a node imports it in its first lines, under `# heavy subsystem: in-body per policy`. At module level `ScriptLine` and `RemoteError` are annotations only, under `TYPE_CHECKING` (`from __future__ import annotations` is already at the top of E's module). `magent --help` therefore never pays for `remote_mux`. `config` is a leaf, so `runs_on_node` stays top-level. If E's module already imports `remote_mux` at the top, move that import in-body in this commit too.

Then add the code below after E's group definition. F defines no group and no heartbeat name; it adds its commands to E's `node_group`.

The heartbeat has ONE reader, E's `node_cmd._daemon_state()` (DECISION-17 as corrected). It judges `log.heartbeat_age(node_sync.HEARTBEAT_NAME)`, where `node_sync.HEARTBEAT_NAME` is `"node-sync"`, written every 10 s and cleared on a clean stop.
- None means `"stopped"`.
- An age of at most `log.HEARTBEAT_MAX_AGE` means `"ok"`.
- Anything older means `"stale"`. A crash leaves the file behind, so it lands here and gives the right `warn`.

`sync_lines` calls `_daemon_state()` and maps its three answers onto rows. It never computes the verdict itself and never names the heartbeat.

```python
# One mark per row status, shared by node setup and node doctor.
_ROW_MARKS: dict[str, tuple[str, str]] = {
    "ok": ("+", "green"),
    "did": ("+", "cyan"),
    "skip": ("-", "white"),
    "drop": ("-", "yellow"),
    "warn": ("!", "yellow"),
    "fail": ("x", "red"),
    "key": ("*", "cyan"),
}


def _refuse(message: str, *, as_json: bool = False) -> NoReturn:
    """A request magent cannot act on (unknown nick, bad argument): exit 2,
    before anything reaches a node."""
    if as_json:
        click.echo(json.dumps({"ok": False, "error": message}))
    else:
        click.echo(f"Error: {message}", err=True)
    sys.exit(2)


def _unreachable(node: nodes.Node, exc: RemoteError) -> ScriptLine:
    """ssh's own failure as a row: the target and ssh's last stderr line."""
    # heavy subsystem: in-body per policy (remote_mux: ssh/tar; --help never pays)
    from magent.remote_mux import ScriptLine

    tail = exc.stderr_tail.strip().splitlines()
    why = tail[-1] if tail else f"rc={exc.rc}"
    return ScriptLine("fail", "reach", f"cannot reach {node.target}: {why}")


def _print_rows(lines: Sequence[ScriptLine]) -> None:
    width = max((len(line.item) for line in lines), default=0)
    for line in lines:
        mark, color = _ROW_MARKS.get(line.status, ("?", "white"))
        quiet = line.status in ("ok", "skip")
        click.echo(
            f"    {style(mark, fg=color, bold=True)} {line.item:<{width}}  "
            f"{style(line.detail, dim=quiet)}"
        )


def sync_lines(cfg: MagentConfig, nick: str, *, now: float) -> list[ScriptLine]:
    """This PC's half of a node's health: is the sync daemon alive, and how old
    is the sessions snapshot it last pulled from ``nick``. Reads only."""
    # heavy subsystem: in-body per policy (remote_mux: ssh/tar; --help never pays)
    from magent.remote_mux import ScriptLine

    lines: list[ScriptLine] = []
    state = _daemon_state()
    if state == "ok":
        lines.append(ScriptLine("ok", "sync-daemon", "running"))
    elif state == "stale":
        lines.append(
            ScriptLine(
                "warn", "sync-daemon", "its heartbeat is stale -- see: magent status"
            )
        )
    elif any(runs_on_node(p) for p in cfg.projects):
        lines.append(
            ScriptLine(
                "warn",
                "sync-daemon",
                "not running -- magent serve starts it while a project runs on a node",
            )
        )
    else:
        lines.append(ScriptLine("skip", "sync-daemon", "no project runs on a node"))
    # E's reader and E's staleness rule; F does not parse sessions.json itself.
    snap = nodes.read_sessions(nick)
    interval = cfg.settings.node_sync.pull_interval_s
    limit = 2 * interval
    age = 0.0 if snap is None else max(0.0, now - snap.ts)
    if snap is None:
        lines.append(
            ScriptLine("skip", "snapshot", "no sessions snapshot from this node yet")
        )
    elif not nodes.sessions_stale(snap, pull_interval_s=interval, now=now):
        lines.append(ScriptLine("ok", "snapshot", f"pulled {age:.0f}s ago"))
    else:
        lines.append(
            ScriptLine(
                "warn",
                "snapshot",
                (
                    f"pulled {age:.0f}s ago, older than 2 x pullIntervalS ({limit}s): "
                    "its sessions read stale"
                ),
            )
        )
    return lines


def node_checks(cfg: MagentConfig, nick: str, *, now: float) -> list[ScriptLine]:
    """Every health row for ``nick``: the node's own (doctor.sh, one ssh call),
    then this PC's sync rows. An unreachable node is one ``fail reach`` row;
    nothing here raises. Read-only: it never provisions (DECISION-24 wires
    provisioning into ``node setup`` and the bring-up, not the doctor)."""
    # heavy subsystem: in-body per policy (remote_mux: ssh/tar; --help never pays)
    from magent import remote_mux
    from magent.remote_mux import ScriptLine

    try:
        node = nodes.node_for_nick(cfg, nick, local_user=env.local_username())
    except nodes.NodeConfigError as exc:
        return [ScriptLine("fail", "config", str(exc))]
    try:
        remote = list(
            remote_mux.doctor(node, timeout_s=remote_mux.DOCTOR_TIMEOUT_S).lines
        )
    except remote_mux.RemoteError as exc:
        remote = [_unreachable(node, exc)]
    return [*remote, *sync_lines(cfg, nick, now=now)]


def doctor_report(cfg: MagentConfig, nicks: list[str]) -> dict[str, list[ScriptLine]]:
    """``node_checks`` for each nick, concurrently -- one ssh each, so N nodes
    cost one DOCTOR_TIMEOUT_S -- keyed in the order given."""
    if not nicks:
        return {}
    now = time.time()
    with ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=len(nicks)) as pool:
        results = list(pool.map(lambda n: node_checks(cfg, n, now=now), nicks))
    return dict(zip(nicks, results, strict=True))


@node_group.command("doctor")
@click.argument("nick", required=False)
@click.option("--json", "as_json", is_flag=True, help="Print the rows as JSON")
@click.pass_context
def node_doctor_cmd(ctx: click.Context, nick: str | None, as_json: bool) -> None:
    """Check a node, or every node: tools, the Claude login, the node's GitHub
    key, locale, disk, and this PC's sync daemon and snapshot.

    Exit 0 when nothing failed (warnings allowed), 1 when a check failed,
    2 when NICK is not in settings.nodes.
    """
    cfg = _load_config_or_exit(find_config(ctx.obj.get("config_path")), as_json=as_json)
    if nick is not None:
        try:
            nodes.node_for_nick(cfg, nick, local_user=env.local_username())
        except nodes.NodeConfigError as exc:
            _refuse(str(exc), as_json=as_json)
    nicks = [nick] if nick is not None else list(cfg.settings.nodes)
    report = doctor_report(cfg, nicks)
    failures = sum(
        1 for lines in report.values() for line in lines if line.status == "fail"
    )
    if as_json:
        body = {
            n: [dataclasses.asdict(line) for line in lines]
            for n, lines in report.items()
        }
        click.echo(json.dumps({"ok": True, "failures": failures, "nodes": body}))
        sys.exit(1 if failures else 0)
    if not nicks:
        click.echo(
            f"  {style('-', dim=True)} no nodes configured -- add one under"
            " settings.nodes, then run: magent node setup <nick>"
        )
        return
    click.echo(f"  {style('magent node doctor', bold=True)}")
    for n, lines in report.items():
        click.echo()
        click.echo(
            f"  {style(n, bold=True)}  {style(cfg.settings.nodes[n].host, dim=True)}"
        )
        _print_rows(lines)
    click.echo()
    if failures:
        click.echo(f"  {style(f'{failures} check(s) failed.', fg='red', bold=True)}")
        sys.exit(1)
    click.echo(f"  {style('No failures.', fg='green', bold=True)}")
```

If that row is absent, add it immediately after the `("magent peek <session>", (...)),` row, which is E's own anchor (DECISION-25: each PR lands its own docs, whatever merged before it). ---

### Task 17: `magent node setup <nick> [--user U]... [--key F]`

The whole first-time flow, in order:
1. **Validate locally; nothing is sent when this fails (exit 2).** The nick resolves through `node_for_nick`. Each `--user` (default: the node's own user) must match setup.sh's user rule. The public key is `--key F`, or the first of `~/.ssh/id_ed25519.pub`, `id_ecdsa.pub`, `id_rsa.pub`. It must be one public-key line. A file holding `PRIVATE KEY` is refused, and its content is never printed.
2. **`setup_node`, as `root@<host>`, this once.** An unreachable root login exits 1. The message names the check to run: `ssh root@<host> true` must work without a password prompt, because every node call runs with `BatchMode=yes`.
3. **`register_ssh_key`** for each user that came back with a `key` row. The title is `magent <user>@<host>`.
4. **Per user with a key: `remote_mux.provision_node(..., force=True)` (DECISION-24: the same body the bring-up calls), then `doctor`.** A user without a key row (their phase failed) goes no further; their `fail` row already says why.
5. **The Claude login stays manual (D5).** When a user's `claude-login` row is not `ok`, the last lines say `last step, by hand, once: ssh <user>@<host> claude`. That row is the ONE `fail` that does not fail setup: it is the documented manual step, not a broken one. Any other `fail` row exits 1.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/magent/cli/node_cmd.py`.
- Modify: `src/magent/cli/docs.py`: the `magent node setup ...` row.
- Test: `tests/unit/test_node_cmd.py` (append; add `from pathlib import Path`, `remote_mux` to the `from magent import ...` line, and `from tests.unit._fake_ssh import gh_auth_status`).

```python
PC_KEY = "ssh-ed25519 AAAAC3NzaC1lZDI1NTE5AAAAIFAKEPCKEY me@pc"
NODE_KEY = "ssh-ed25519 AAAAFAKENODEKEY magent@box-second"
NOT_LOGGED_IN = (
    "fail\tclaude-login\tClaude Code is not logged in here -- "
    "run once: ssh alice@box-second claude\n"
)


def _pc_key(name: str = "id_ed25519.pub", text: str = PC_KEY + "\n") -> Path:
    path = Path.home() / ".ssh" / name
    path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    path.write_text(text, encoding="utf-8")
    return path


def _node_answers(fake_ssh, *, users=("alice",), setup_extra="", doctor=NOT_LOGGED_IN):
    """The fake node: root's setup call reports a key per user; provision
    (`--force`) installs the hook; doctor (`--target`) answers ``doctor``."""
    keys = "".join(
        f"key\t{u}\tssh-ed25519 AAAA{u.upper()} magent@box-second\n" for u in users
    )
    fake_ssh.set_reply(
        "root@box-second", stdout=f"did\tpackages\tinstalled\n{setup_extra}{keys}"
    )
    fake_ssh.set_reply(
        "--force", stdout="did\tstate_hook\t~/.magent/bin/state-hook.sh\n"
    )
    fake_ssh.set_reply("--target", stdout="ok\ttmux\ttmux 3.4\n" + doctor)


def _gh_can_add_keys(fake_gh) -> None:
    fake_gh.set_reply(
        "auth status", stdout=gh_auth_status("alice", "admin:public_key, repo")
    )


def _setup(runner, cfg: str, *args: str):
    return runner.invoke(cli.main, ["--config", cfg, "node", "setup", *args])


def _targets(fake_ssh) -> list[str]:
    return [next(a for a in c.argv if "@box-" in a) for c in fake_ssh.calls()]


class TestNodeSetup:
    def test_it_sets_up_as_root_then_provisions_and_checks_as_the_user(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh, fake_gh
    ):
        _pc_key()
        _node_answers(fake_ssh)
        _gh_can_add_keys(fake_gh)
        _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second")
        calls = fake_ssh.calls()
        assert _targets(fake_ssh) == [
            "root@box-second",
            "alice@box-second",
            "alice@box-second",
        ]
        assert "--force" in calls[1].argv[-1]
        assert "--target" in calls[2].argv[-1]

    def test_this_pcs_public_key_is_the_setup_payload(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh

        _pc_key()
        _node_answers(fake_ssh)
        _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second")
        assert fake_ssh.calls()[0].stdin.endswith((PC_KEY + "\n").encode("ascii"))

    def test_the_nodes_key_is_registered_under_a_title_naming_it(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh, fake_gh
    ):
        _pc_key()
        _node_answers(fake_ssh)
        _gh_can_add_keys(fake_gh)
        _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second")
        (add,) = [c for c in fake_gh.calls() if c.argv[:2] == ["ssh-key", "add"]]
        assert add.argv[add.argv.index("--title") + 1] == "magent alice@box-second"
        assert add.stdin == b"ssh-ed25519 AAAAAMIN magent@box-second\n"

        assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
        assert "last step, by hand, once: ssh alice@box-second claude" in result.stdout

    def test_a_logged_in_node_gets_no_reminder(

        assert result.exit_code == 0, result.output
        assert "last step" not in result.stdout

    def test_any_other_failed_step_exits_one(

    def test_each_user_is_created_and_then_provisioned_as_itself(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh, fake_gh

        )

    def test_the_default_user_is_the_nodes_user(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        _pc_key()

        other = _pc_key("work.pub", "ssh-ed25519 AAAAWORKKEY me@work\n")
        _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second", "--key", str(other))
        assert fake_ssh.calls()[0].stdin.endswith(b"ssh-ed25519 AAAAWORKKEY me@work\n")


class TestNodeSetupRefusesBeforeAnySsh:

        result = _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second")
        assert result.exit_code == 2

    def test_a_private_key_is_refused_and_never_printed(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh
    ):
        secret = "b3BlbnNzDECOYXktdjEAAAAABG5vbmUAAAAEbm9uZQ"
        path = _pc_key(
            "id_ed25519",
            f"-----BEGIN OPENSSH PRIVATE KEY-----\n{secret}\n-----END OPENSSH PRIVATE KEY-----\n",
        )
        result = _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second", "--key", str(path))
        assert result.exit_code == 2
        assert "PRIVATE key" in result.output
        assert secret not in result.output
        assert fake_ssh.calls() == []

    def test_a_bad_user_name_is_refused(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        _pc_key()
        result = _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second", "--user", "Bob")
        assert result.exit_code == 2
        assert "not a valid Unix user name: Bob" in result.output
        assert fake_ssh.calls() == []

    def test_an_unknown_nick_names_the_pool(self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh):
        _pc_key()
        result = _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "nope")
        assert result.exit_code == 2
        assert "node 'nope' is not in settings.nodes (known: second)" in result.output
        assert fake_ssh.calls() == []


class TestNodeSetupUnreachable:
    def test_an_unreachable_root_login_exits_one_and_names_the_check(
        self, runner, tmp_config, fake_ssh, fake_gh
    ):
        _pc_key()
        fake_ssh.set_reply(
            "root@box-second",
            stderr="root@box-second: Permission denied (publickey).\n",
            rc=255,
        )
        result = _setup(runner, _pool_file(tmp_config), "second")
        assert result.exit_code == 1
        assert "Permission denied (publickey)." in result.stdout
        assert "ssh root@box-second true" in result.stdout
        assert len(fake_ssh.calls()) == 1
        assert fake_gh.calls() == []
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `src/magent/cli/node_cmd.py`, add `import re` and `from pathlib import Path` to the top-level imports (`remote_mux` stays in-body, Task 16), then add after `node_doctor_cmd`:

```python
# The Unix user rule setup.sh enforces, checked here too so a typo costs no ssh.
_USER_RE = re.compile(r"^[a-z_][a-z0-9_-]{0,31}$")
_DEFAULT_PUBKEYS = ("id_ed25519.pub", "id_ecdsa.pub", "id_rsa.pub")


def _pubkey(key_file: Path | None) -> str:
    """This PC's public key line: ``key_file``, or the first default under
    ~/.ssh. Raises ValueError with the message to print; the file's content
    is never part of it."""
    if key_file is None:
        ssh_dir = Path.home() / ".ssh"
        key_file = next(
            (ssh_dir / name for name in _DEFAULT_PUBKEYS if (ssh_dir / name).is_file()),
            None,
        )
        if key_file is None:
            raise ValueError(
                f"no public key in {ssh_dir} -- pass one: --key <file.pub>"
            )
    try:
        text = key_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    except (OSError, UnicodeDecodeError) as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"cannot read {key_file}: {type(exc).__name__}") from exc
    if "PRIVATE KEY" in text:
        raise ValueError(
            f"{key_file} is a PRIVATE key -- pass its .pub file with --key"
        )
    lines = [line.strip() for line in text.splitlines() if line.strip()]
    if len(lines) != 1 or len(lines[0].split()) < 2:
        raise ValueError(f"{key_file} is not one ssh public key line")
    return lines[0]


def _provision_and_check(node: nodes.Node, cfg: MagentConfig) -> list[ScriptLine]:
    """A freshly set-up user: the whole user scope (forced), then the node's
    health rows. An unreachable user login is one ``fail reach`` row. The
    scope is built by ``remote_mux.provision_node``, THE provisioning body
    (DECISION-24) -- the same call the bring-up makes, so setup and ``--go``
    can never ship different scopes."""
    # heavy subsystem: in-body per policy (remote_mux: ssh/tar; --help never pays)
    from magent import remote_mux

    try:
        rows = list(
            remote_mux.provision_node(
                node,
                cfg,
                home=Path.home(),
                timeout_s=remote_mux.PROVISION_TIMEOUT_S,
                force=True,
            ).lines
        )
    except remote_mux.RemoteError as exc:
        return [_unreachable(node, exc)]
    try:
        rows += remote_mux.doctor(node, timeout_s=remote_mux.DOCTOR_TIMEOUT_S).lines
    except remote_mux.RemoteError as exc:
        rows.append(_unreachable(node, exc))
    return rows


@node_group.command("setup")
@click.argument("nick")
@click.option(
    "--user",
    "users",
    multiple=True,
    help="A Unix user to create on the node (repeatable; default: the node's user).",
)
@click.option(
    "--key",
    "key_file",
    type=click.Path(dir_okay=False, path_type=Path),
    help="This PC's ssh PUBLIC key to authorize (default: ~/.ssh/id_ed25519.pub, then id_ecdsa, id_rsa).",
)
@click.pass_context
def node_setup_cmd(
    ctx: click.Context, nick: str, users: tuple[str, ...], key_file: Path | None
) -> None:
    """Prepare a machine once: packages, a per-person user, your key, Claude
    Code and the node's own GitHub key, then the user scope and a check.

    Logs in as root@<host> for this one hop. Idempotent: every step prints
    ok/did/skip. The Claude login is NOT copied: run `ssh <user>@<host> claude`
    once. Exit 0 when only that login is left, 1 when a step failed, 2 when
    nothing was sent (unknown nick, bad user name, no public key).
    """
    # heavy subsystem: in-body per policy (remote_mux: ssh/tar; --help never pays)
    from magent import remote_mux

    cfg = _load_config_or_exit(find_config(ctx.obj.get("config_path")))
    try:
        node = nodes.node_for_nick(cfg, nick, local_user=env.local_username())
        pubkey = _pubkey(key_file)
    except ValueError as exc:
        _refuse(str(exc))
    names = list(users) or [node.user]
    for name in names:
        if not _USER_RE.match(name):
            _refuse(f"not a valid Unix user name: {name}")

    click.echo(
        f"  {style('magent node setup', bold=True)} {nick}  "
        f"{style(f'as root@{node.host}, this once', dim=True)}"
    )
    try:
        report = remote_mux.setup_node(
            node, names, pubkey, timeout_s=remote_mux.SETUP_TIMEOUT_S
        )
    except remote_mux.RemoteError as exc:
        _print_rows([_unreachable(dataclasses.replace(node, user="root"), exc)])
        click.echo(
            f"    setup logs in as root once, with this PC's ssh key: check that "
            f"`ssh root@{node.host} true` works without a password prompt"
        )
        sys.exit(1)
    rows = [line for line in report.lines if line.status != "key"]
    _print_rows(rows)

    keys = report.keys()
    ready = [name for name in names if name in keys]
    github = [
        remote_mux.register_ssh_key(keys[name], title=f"magent {name}@{node.host}")
        for name in ready
    ]
    if github:
        click.echo()
        click.echo(f"  {style('GitHub', bold=True)}")
        _print_rows(github)
    rows += github

    no_login: list[str] = []
    for name in ready:
        user_node = dataclasses.replace(node, user=name)
        click.echo()
        click.echo(f"  {style(user_node.target, bold=True)}")
        checked = _provision_and_check(user_node, cfg)
        _print_rows(checked)
        rows += checked
        if not any(r.item == "claude-login" and r.status == "ok" for r in checked):
            no_login.append(user_node.target)

    # The Claude login is manual by design (D5): its row is the one fail that
    # is a step still to take, not a step that broke.
    failed = [r for r in rows if r.status == "fail" and r.item != "claude-login"]
    click.echo()
    for target in no_login:
        click.echo(
            f"  {style('!', fg='yellow', bold=True)} last step, by hand, once: "
            f"ssh {target} claude  {style('(log in; magent never copies it)', dim=True)}"
        )
    if failed:
        click.echo(f"  {style(f'{len(failed)} step(s) failed.', fg='red', bold=True)}")
        sys.exit(1)
    click.echo(f"  {style('Ready.', fg='green', bold=True)}")
```

`node_cmd` builds no user scope itself: `nodes.user_scope(` appears nowhere in `src/magent/cli/`. The one call is in `remote_mux.provision_node`, pinned by Task 12A's src-wide pin (K moves that pin with its swap).

In `src/magent/cli/docs.py`, add H's row immediately after E's `_CLI_COMMAND_DOCS` row, whose command string is exactly `"magent node sync -d [--once] [--stop]"`, so it lands before the `doctor` row from Task 16. ### Task 18: the `nodes` row in `magent doctor` (WARN at worst)

`magent doctor` gains one row, `nodes`, appended after `upload port`. It folds `node_cmd.doctor_report` (the same rows `magent node doctor` prints, concurrently, one ssh per node) into one line:
- no nodes configured, or no config: `ok`, "no nodes configured";
- every row of every node `ok`/`skip`: `ok`, "N node(s) healthy";
- otherwise `warn`, naming each node's troubled items, e.g. "second: claude-login; fifth: reach -- details: magent node doctor".

It is never `fail`. A node that is down, or not set up yet, is not this machine's environment failing, and `magent doctor`'s exit code stays about this machine. With nodes configured, `magent doctor` now costs up to one `DOCTOR_TIMEOUT_S` (30 s) when a node does not answer.

**Files:**
- Modify: `src/magent/cli/doctor.py`: `_check_nodes` after `_check_upload_port`, and its row in `_run_checks`.
- Test: `tests/unit/test_doctor.py`:
  - add `_check_nodes` to the `from magent.cli.doctor import (...)` list, and `from magent.remote_mux import ScriptLine`;
  - add `"nodes"` to the name set in `test_real_checks_run_end_to_end` (after `"upload port"`);
  - append the class below.

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement.** In `src/magent/cli/doctor.py`, extend the module docstring's list ("… is the upload port sane, are the nodes healthy.") and add after `_check_upload_port`:

```python
def _check_nodes(cfg: MagentConfig | None) -> CheckResult:
    """Every configured node folded into one row -- WARN at worst: a node that
    is down or not set up yet is not this machine's environment failing. The
    per-node rows are `magent node doctor`'s; this reads the same report."""
    if cfg is None or not cfg.settings.nodes:
        return (OK, "no nodes configured")
    # A sibling command module, imported in-body so doctor.py's import never
    # depends on the registration hub's import order.
    from magent.cli import node_cmd

    report = node_cmd.doctor_report(cfg, list(cfg.settings.nodes))
    troubled = [
        f"{nick}: {', '.join(line.item for line in lines if line.status in (FAIL, WARN))}"
        for nick, lines in report.items()
        if any(line.status in (FAIL, WARN) for line in lines)
    ]
    if not troubled:
        return (OK, f"{len(report)} node(s) healthy")
    return (WARN, "; ".join(troubled) + " -- details: magent node doctor")
```

In `_run_checks`, after `("upload port", lambda: _check_upload_port(cfg)),`:

```python
(("nodes", lambda: _check_nodes(cfg)),)
```

### Task 19: F lands its own docs: README, CLAUDE.md, DESIGN.md §2 + ledger rows, CHANGELOG (DECISION-25)

Under DECISION-25 the PR that ships a behaviour writes its docs in its own commit, and H only reconciles. Every step here is **skip-if-present**. Each step starts with the grep that decides, names the heading it anchors on, creates that heading when it is absent, and appends under it when it is present. B, D and E merge before F and may already have created `## Nodes`, `## [3.20.0] - UNRELEASED` and `### The node is a checkout, not a mirror (2026-09-24)`. F may also be re-run after a rebase. L's Task 11 is the template.

- [ ] **Step 1: README (skip-if-present).**

Run: `grep -n "^\*\*Set a machine up once.\*\*" README.md` and `grep -n "^## Nodes$" README.md`
- The first grep hits: skip to Step 2.
- `## Nodes` is absent: insert `## Nodes`, a blank line, and the block below, followed by one blank line, immediately before the line `## Usage`.
- `## Nodes` is present: insert the block below as the FIRST content under it (setup comes before bring-up), with one blank line before and after it. If a `### ` subsection already follows the heading directly, the block still goes above it.

````markdown
**Set a machine up once.** `magent node setup gpu --user you` connects as root one time. It installs tmux, git, curl and python3, creates your own Unix user, authorizes your SSH key, installs Claude Code and `gh` for that user, generates a GitHub key *on the node* (it never leaves the machine) and registers it to your GitHub account, then checks everything. Each step prints `ok`, `did` or `skip`, so running it again is safe. Every session afterwards runs as your user, never as root.

Registering the node's GitHub key needs one extra permission on this PC's `gh` login, which a default login does not have. The first `node setup` stops at its `github-key` step and tells you to run this once:

```bash
gh auth refresh -h github.com -s admin:public_key
```

**Log in to Claude on each node yourself, once:**

```bash
ssh you@gpu-box.example.com claude
```

This is the one thing magent does not copy. A Claude login can only be held by one machine at a time; copying it would log out both this PC and the node. Everything else your sessions need comes across at every bring-up, and an unchanged item costs nothing: your `gh` login, your Claude settings (minus any hook whose command is not installed on the node), your plugins, your skills, and magent's own state hook. Your user-level MCP servers come across when they can work from there: a remote `http`/`sse` server with its login, or a stdio server whose program the node has. A server that runs on this PC (a Windows path, or on `localhost`) stays behind, and the reason is printed. Secrets travel over the SSH connection's input, never on a command line.

| Command | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `magent node setup <nick> [--user U]... [--key F]` | Prepare a machine (see above). `--key` picks the public key to authorize; the default is your `~/.ssh/id_ed25519.pub`, or `id_rsa.pub`. |
| `magent node doctor [<nick>] [--json]` | Check tmux, git, claude and gh on the node, the Claude login, the node's GitHub key, the locale, free disk, and the sync daemon. Read-only; `magent doctor` shows the same as one `nodes` row. |
````

- [ ] **Step 2: CLAUDE.md (skip-if-present).**

Run: `grep -n "No test resolves the real \`gh\`" CLAUDE.md`
- A hit: skip to Step 3.
- No hit: insert this bullet immediately before the bullet that starts `- Tests: JSON-body assertions read \`result.stdout\``:

```markdown
- **No test resolves the real `gh`, and a node is provisioned through ONE body.** `tests/conftest.py::_no_real_gh` (autouse, every tier) makes `remote_mux.find_gh()` answer None, so a test without the `fake_gh` fixture sees "gh not installed". Same family as `_no_real_ssh`: the real `gh` holds the developer's GitHub token, and `node setup` registers an ssh key to their account with it. No HOME redirect contains a binary on PATH. `remote_mux.provision_node(node, config, *, home, timeout_s, force=False)` is the one provisioning body and holds src's ONE `nodes.user_scope(` call (DECISION-24; pinned src-wide in `tests/unit/test_node_provision.py`). `launch._provision_once` calls it once per node per process from the bring-up, recording the node only after a provision that reached it; `magent node setup` calls it with `force=True`; `magent node doctor` never provisions. A `fail` row never blocks a session (it is logged to `"nodes"` at WARNING); an unreachable node fails only that project's `NodeBringUpOutcome`. The gh token rides stdin after `PAYLOAD_SENTINEL`, never argv, and ships only when this PC's `gh` names its account; the Claude login (`claudeAiOauth`) is never shipped. Every node script is sent as `bash -s -- <remote_mux.SOCKET> <args>`, and `doctor.sh` has no socket default of its own (DECISION-26 ii). Rationale: DESIGN.md §2 "The node is a checkout, not a mirror".
```

- [ ] **Step 3: DESIGN.md §2 paragraphs (skip-if-present).**

Run: `grep -n "^\*\*Auth crosses; the Claude login does not (D5).\*\*" DESIGN.md` and `grep -n "^### The node is a checkout, not a mirror (2026-09-24)$" DESIGN.md`
- The first grep hits: skip to Step 4.
- The heading is absent: insert the heading, a blank line, the block below, and one blank line immediately before the line `## 3. Known debt`.
- The heading is present: append the block below at the end of that entry (immediately before the next line starting `### ` or `## `), with one blank line before and after it. If a paragraph with one of these bold leads already exists, skip that paragraph and keep the others.

````markdown
**One person, one Unix user; root only for the hop that creates it (D4).**
`magent node setup` uses root once, to install packages, create the user and
authorize this PC's key. Every session after that runs as a dedicated
per-person user (`alice`, `bob`). On a machine other people also use, that is
what keeps credentials apart, bounds the blast radius of an agent's `rm`,
attributes every process to someone, and matches the per-user state layout
Claude Code already assumes. Sessions run as root only when `"user": "root"` is
written into that node's config, and even then `load_config` warns on every
load.

**Auth crosses; the Claude login does not (D5).** Every bring-up provisions the
node user's scope one way from this PC, content-hashed so an unchanged item
costs nothing: the `gh` token, `~/.claude/settings.json`, the user-scope MCP
servers that can work from there and their OAuth entries, the plugin list, the
skills directory, and magent's own node-side state hook wired with the same
event map as `magent hooks install`. Filters decide what "can work" means, and
each drop is printed with its reason, never silent. A hook whose command does
not resolve on the node is dropped, because a hook that cannot run fails every
turn. An MCP server is classified by transport (`nodes.mcp_skip_reason` is the
one classifier): a remote `http`/`sse` server ships; one on this PC's loopback,
or a stdio server whose command is a path on this PC, stays behind; any other
stdio server ships only if the node's `command -v` finds its program, which one
extra ssh call asks before the payload is built, so a dropped server's `env`
never leaves this PC. An MCP OAuth token the node has since refreshed is never
overwritten. The gh token ships only when this PC's `gh` names its account. The
Claude login is the one deliberate exception and is done by hand, once per node
(`ssh <user>@<host> claude`). Its refresh token has a single holder, and two
machines refreshing the same token log each other out. That is also why the
Claude OAuth block in `.credentials.json` is never copied, and neither are
`~/.ssh`, `ANTHROPIC_API_KEY` or anything under `~/.claude-swap-backup`. Secrets
travel on stdin, never in an argv (any user on a shared node can read argv from
`/proc`) and never in a log: `RemoteError` carries its command with the stdin
payload elided, and a unit test greps every argv the fake ssh recorded for the
fake token.

**One provisioning body, called once per node per process (DECISION-24).**
`remote_mux.provision_node` builds the scope and provisions; it is the only
place `src/` builds a user scope, so the relay (plan K) swaps one line and the
bring-up, `node setup` and every later caller follow. The bring-up calls it
through `launch._provision_once` under the node's lock, after every refusal, so
a refused project provisions nothing and `--dry-run` only says it would. A node
counts as provisioned only once a provision reached it: an unreachable node
fails that one project and the next project on it tries again. A `fail` row
(a plugin that would not install) does not stop the session; it is logged, and
`magent node doctor` shows the node's state. The digest store on the node makes
a second provision nearly free, so once per process is a cost bound, not a
correctness one.

**The node pushes with its own key (D6).** `node setup` generates an ed25519
key in the node user's home, where it stays, and registers it to the user's
GitHub account with `gh ssh-key add`. Agent forwarding was the easy answer and
the wrong one. A push has to work with no window attached, after this PC sleeps,
and from a bring-up started by the Session-0 hand-off, and none of those has an
agent to forward. A forwarded agent on a shared machine also lends this PC's key
to anyone with root there. Registering needs the `admin:public_key` scope on this
PC's `gh` login, which a default login lacks. `register_ssh_key` never raises:
the first real `node setup` stops at its `github-key` step and names the one-time
fix, `gh auth refresh -h github.com -s admin:public_key`.
````

- [ ] **Step 4: DESIGN.md §3 rows (skip-if-present, per row).**

Run: `grep -n "^\*\*MCP OAuth refresh tokens are shipped to a node" DESIGN.md`
- A hit: the rows are there; skip to Step 5.
- No hit: insert the block below, followed by one blank line, immediately before the line that starts `**Attach-pane reconnect is only reachable from a Windows client (2026-08-09):**`.

````markdown
**MCP OAuth refresh tokens are shipped to a node (2026-09-24):** they face the
same single-holder hazard as the Claude login: if a server rotates refresh
tokens, whichever machine refreshes first invalidates the other copies. Only
entries for servers the node actually has are merged, and a token the node has
since refreshed is never overwritten. If this bites, stop shipping `mcpOAuth`
and log in per node.

**`settings.env.DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER` ships with the PC's settings (2026-09-24):**
settings ship whole, minus the never-shipped keys, so this PC's choice also
freezes the node's natively-installed Claude Code.

**A running `claude` on a node can lose a provision's `mcpServers` merge
(2026-09-24):** `node_apply` writes `~/.claude.json` at 0600 through a tmp file
and `os.replace`, so no reader sees half a file, but a Claude process that
rewrites the whole file between node_apply's read and write drops the merge.
The next provision re-applies it, because the node's digest store only records
a merge that was present.

**`~/.claude/plugins/known_marketplaces.json` is an undocumented internal file
(2026-09-24):** it is read defensively. A marketplace with no remote source
there or in `settings.extraKnownMarketplaces` becomes a printed note, not an
error.

**`setup.sh` assumes a Debian/Ubuntu node (2026-09-24):** apt and dpkg. A
non-apt node fails the `packages` row loudly. The gh apt-repository branch
writes `/etc` and is not unit-tested (the unit tier's shims put a `gh` on
PATH); only a real node proves it.

**Settings keys removed on this PC stay on the node (2026-09-24):** the merge is
`{**node, **pc}`, one way and additive. `hooks` is the exception and is rebuilt
from the PC's on every apply.

**`gh` on a headless node keeps its token in `~/.config/gh/hosts.yml`
(2026-09-24):** in plaintext when the node has no keyring, as for any
`gh auth login` there. magent itself never writes the token to a file and
never puts it in an argv.
````
